Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a potentials algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 402. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 786/5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the potentials space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 402 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 786. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on potentials structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 88. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 209 with relations in degree 4.

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
